# Rust moves: TypeScript logic the iPhone app cannot run

Read from StreamNook dev 577f3c02 + working tree of 2026-10-12 (the Android phone shell in `src/mobile`, the shared stores and services in `src`, and the Rust core). This is the phase 3 input of the iPhone plan (`C:\Users\Brandon\.claude\plans\streamnook-ios\PLAN.md`): every piece of real logic (deciding, ranking, filtering, merging, keying, polling, caching, parsing, retry policy, timers, state machines) that the phone path runs in TypeScript today, with the Rust home it should move to. The native SwiftUI app only presents, so anything left here is something Swift would have to rewrite.

"Rust first" applies to every move: the Rust version replaces the TypeScript one on desktop and Android in the same change, and sibling surfaces are fixed in that change too. The parity contract that depends on these moves is [`parity/README.md`](parity/README.md).

Path shorthand used in the citations:

- `m:` is `src/mobile/` (the Android phone shell)
- `s:` is `src/` outside `src/mobile/` (shared stores, services, utils, styles)
- `d:` is desktop code under `src/` consulted for RULE ZERO (usually `src/components/`)
- `r:` is `src-tauri/src/` (the Rust core)
- `k:` is `src-tauri/gen/android/app/src/main/java/app/streamnook/` (the Android Kotlin layer)
- `t:` is `src-tauri/` outside `src/` (config, Android resources)
- `p:` is the repository root
- `pref:` is a file in the StreamNook memory folder (`C:\Users\Brandon\.claude\projects\C--StreamNook\memory\`): Brandon's standing preferences and recorded traps

Line format:

```markdown
- [ ] <what it decides> (citation) -> <Rust home> | callers: desktop <files> / Android-mobile <files> | risk: <level + why> | keys: <key space> [P1]
```

- `r:` paths exist today. A home written as new `services/<name>.rs` is a module proposed here.
- "Duplicate of r:..." means Rust already does the job: delete the TypeScript copy in the same change.
- **P1** marks logic the Swift app cannot avoid needing on its main paths (boot, follows, browse, watch, chat, emotes, cosmetics on chat rows, badges, rewards, accounts). Do the P1 items of an area before its Swift builder starts.
- `[~] WebView-only` marks hls.js or WebView workarounds that simply disappear on a native player; they need no Rust home.
- "Stays in Swift" lines are view state Swift must mirror, listed so nobody moves them.
- Key spaces follow Brain `references/StreamNook_Identity_Keying.md`: chat key (bare Twitch user id, `kick:<numeric id>`, `youtube:<UC id>`), member id (always a Twitch user id), chat slice key (bare lowercased Twitch login, `provider:channel`), persisted `streamKey` (bare Twitch login, `kick:<slug>`), runtime `makeKey` (`twitch:login`), 7TV user id. Never mix them; each item names the one it uses, and the key-space bugs found during the audit are listed in Part 1 under "Key-space bugs found".

## Top 15 by priority

Ordered by what the Swift app reaches first (boot, Following, Watch, chat), then by how much Swift would otherwise have to rewrite. Each names the items below that make it up.

1. **Boot state and settings load.** One Rust boot answer (`needs_setup`, signed-in state, first settings with the never-fall-back-to-defaults rule and the load-time migrations) instead of the page's boot steps and localStorage backstop; also fixes the `setup_complete` revert. Part 1 `stores/AppStore.ts` settings load and save; Part 2 shell-design `boot/useMobileBoot.ts`.
2. **Sign-in, auth status, account switch and the sign-in sheet coordinator.** A typed auth status and sign-in reason in Rust, the post-login sequence run by Rust, and new `services/sign_in_sheet.rs` deciding what the in-app sign-in browser does on every page load (Twitch, Drops, Kick, 7TV, Subscribe), so the Android overlay and the iPhone WKWebView sheet both just follow it. Every sign-out clears the sheet's cookie jar. Part 1 `stores/AppStore.ts` auth items; Part 2 accounts-signin.
3. **Following from the `following` snapshot section.** The phone's merge (`followingMerge.ts`), the provider live-slice races and the hype-train list become Rust's: `r:services/unified_following.rs` already ranks and dedupes; add the phone's provider set and a per-screen hype-train claim. Part 1 `stores/followsStore.ts`, `utils/streamProvider.ts`; Part 2 following-browse-search.
4. **Watch start, stop and restart orchestration.** New `services/watch_start.rs` beside `r:services/watch_session.rs`: start tickets, teardown order, restart policy, and the four places a Kick or YouTube stream is treated as Twitch. Part 1 `stores/AppStore.ts` watch items.
5. **Earning gate, latency path and rate governor.** The playing/paused earning-gate state machine (6 s pause grace), the low-latency path decision with desktop's prefetch probe, the rate governor and its constants, the stall cushion and the behind-live watchdog, so the iPhone reaches desktop latency (about 1.5 to 2 s) from one Rust policy. Part 2 watch-player.
6. **Chat slice to Rust.** New `services/chat_slice.rs`: acquire, release, buffer, flush, holds, history merge, moderation marks. The phone's whole chat pipeline is TypeScript today. Part 1 `stores/chatConnectionStore.ts`.
7. **Chat liveness: reconnect, watchdog and rebuild in the IRC supervisor, plus an `app_lifecycle` service.** Retry and backoff, the stale-watchdog ladder, the single-flight 60 s hard cycle and the 90 s resume rebuild move into Rust, driven by a Rust foreground/background state that every Rust poller reads. Part 1 `stores/chatConnectionStore.ts`; Part 2 chat-view (`chatRecovery.ts`, `useChatWatchdog.ts`), system (`lifecycle.ts`, `backgroundGate.ts`).
8. **Chat send and own-row reconciliation.** One Rust send that pushes the pending row, upgrades it on echo, stamps the Helix id and removes it on rejection, replacing four TypeScript paths. Part 1 `stores/chatConnectionStore.ts`.
9. **The row segment builder.** Mentions, links, emote grouping, zero-width stacking, event and system row text, badge filtering by settings and the badge index move into the Rust segment builder, so the UIKit chat list only draws segments. Part 2 chat-view (`ChatMessage.tsx`, `twitchChat.ts`, `twitchBadges.ts`).
10. **Chatter registry with cosmetics stamped on rows, and the 7TV image picks.** New `services/chatter_registry.rs`; deletes the duplicate TypeScript 7TV cache; 7TV badge and paint picks and profile badge groups in Rust. Part 1 `stores/chatUserStore.ts`, `services/cosmeticsCache.ts`, `services/seventvService.ts`, `utils/profileBadges.ts`; Part 2 profile-cosmetics.
11. **The StreamNook backend client and member registries.** New `services/streamnook_backend.rs`, member and cosmetics registries looked up per chatter, the atmosphere ownership gate and member theme resolve, and every privileged write on the authenticated path (never the shipped public key). Part 1 `services/supabaseService.ts`, `stores/chatUserStore.ts`.
12. **Emotes from `CHANNEL_EMOTES`, and the picker's grouping, search and favourites.** One channel emote set per provider served by Rust, deleting the page cache and its second name index; the picker's provider tabs, owner-name groups, search and favourites computed in Rust. Part 1 `stores/chatConnectionStore.ts`, `services/emoteService.ts`; Part 2 chat-input-emotes.
13. **The slash command dispatcher.** `commandHandler.ts` (about 1,270 lines) with `/nuke` and `/remind`, into `r:services/slash_commands.rs`; also makes `/poll`, `/prediction`, `/clearmessages`, `/usercard` and `/user` work on phones, where they do nothing today. Part 2 chat-input-emotes.
14. **Settings writes as Rust read-modify-write commands.** Every page-built whole-group write (the stale-group bug class) becomes a field patch or a per-entry Rust command; option catalogs, the reset-arrow engine and custom-command conflict checks move with them. Part 2 settings-a, settings-b.
15. **Theme resolution.** New `services/theme.rs`: the 22 palettes, custom themes, the OLED accent math, the glassiness blend (0% is fully opaque) and the status-bar luminance rule, so Swift only receives resolved colours. Part 2 shell-design.

Next after these: channel points and the bonus chest (Rust already auto-claims; the phone's duplicate claim and 60 s poll go), drops eligibility and the per-game campaign list, the badge gallery store, notifications as ready-to-post items from Rust.

## Rust to open up for iOS (cfg and registration gaps found during the audit)

These are not TypeScript moves but block the matching Swift screens; they belong with the core lane's phase 2 work.

- [ ] Ad-free relay and the low-latency stand-down checks compile for Android only (`#[cfg(target_os = "android")] pub mod ad_bypass`); widen to `cfg(mobile)`, since iOS cannot spawn the desktop playback plugin either (r:services/mod.rs:L19-L20) [P1]
- [ ] `fetch_chat_identity_badges_gql` and `update_chat_identity_gql` are Android-only; the Cosmetics screen's Twitch badge choice needs them on iOS: `#[cfg(mobile)]` (r:commands/chat_identity.rs:L378-L400) [P1]
- [ ] Push register and unregister live in the Android-only `android_notify.rs`; split the platform-free collector and dedupe (`collect_all`, `collect_live`, `collect_badges`, `notify_state.json`) from the JNI lanes so APNs registration and a notification service extension can call them (r:android_notify.rs:L18, L426-L484)
- [ ] `export_logs` is Android-only; add an iOS arm that hands the zip to Swift for a Files export or the share sheet (r:commands/logs.rs:L172-L225)
- [ ] Kick import and sign-in are iOS stubs ("Kick account sync is not available on this platform", "Kick sign-in is not available on this platform"); port the Android arms over the sheet's cookie jar fed by `set_web_cookies` (r:services/providers/kick_account.rs:L107-L111, L670-L673) [P1]
- [ ] `open_subscribe_window` is registered for desktop and Android only; iOS needs an arm that opens the checkout in the sign-in sheet (r:commands/twitch.rs:L1342-L1365)
- [ ] The desktop live-notification poll is gated `not(target_os = "android")`, so it would arm on iOS; gate it `cfg(desktop)` (r:lib.rs:L1220-L1223, L1291-L1304)
- [ ] The desktop Twitch cookie harvest compiles on iOS and reads the main webview's (empty) jar through the nested-run-loop cookie API that froze macOS 8.6.2; give iOS its own arm over the sign-in sheet's cookies (r:services/twitch_auth_service.rs:L267)
- [ ] `get_android_changelog` reads the Android update manifest only; What's New on iOS needs an iOS-line twin (source to be decided) (r:services/changelog.rs:L16, L531-L547)
- [ ] The setup wizard's Components page runs `check_components_installed` and `extract_bundled_components` on phones although both are no-ops; drop the page on `cfg(mobile)` (d:components/SetupWizard.tsx:L247-L310)

### Key-space bugs found (fix inside the move, not after)

- [ ] Full-profile cache keyed by Twitch user id alone while its badges depend on the channel: a card opened in channel B can show channel A's sub art (s:services/cosmeticsCache.ts:L412-L496)
- [ ] Curated third-party loadout skipped for every namespaced chat key before the member check, and the resolved-identity bridge looks rows up by Twitch id, so a member claimed on Kick or YouTube never wears their loadout there (s:stores/chatUserStore.ts:L256-L264, L1058-L1065)
- [ ] The phone profile sheet hands the raw chat key to the StreamNook badge while resolving the member number through `memberIdFor` (m:profile/UserProfileSheet.tsx:L246-L251, L323)
- [ ] The phone builds chat keys by hand instead of `chatterId` / `cosmeticsKey`, a second key derivation (m:chat/MobileChatPane.tsx:L300-L312)
- [ ] YouTube emotes cached in the page by the broadcast address, not the UC id Rust's `store_key` uses; HIDE_USERS matches display names against hidden logins; own chat color is one global value across accounts (s:stores/chatConnectionStore.ts:L379-L386, L849-L895, L356-L377)
- [ ] Channel-less handling in chat: NOTICE send rejections delete your pending row in every open chat, NOTICE rows and `twitch-system-message` rows are dropped when two chats are open, Twitch IRC status frames mark Kick and YouTube slices disconnected (s:stores/chatConnectionStore.ts:L2694-L2762, L1385-L1400, L4029-L4046)
- [ ] Provider rows treated as Twitch in the watch path: `unregister_active_channel` gets a Kick numeric id, Alt+X / `/clip` send a Kick or YouTube id as a Helix `broadcasterId`, the lock-screen audio swap resolves `twitch.tv/<kick slug>`, an offline room opens the Twitch account that shares a Kick slug (s:stores/AppStore.ts:L2229-L2232, L1917-L1923, L2504-L2507, L2811-L2909)
- [ ] `startStream` parses keys with `channel.includes(':')` beside `parseKey` (two parsers for one job), and `/reload` passes a bare login so a `kick:slug` slice is never found (s:stores/AppStore.ts:L2604-L2626, s:stores/chatConnectionStore.ts:L1695-L1738)
- [ ] Phone Following merge dedupes on `streamKey`, which the keying note forbids for cross-source merges (YouTube video id vs UC id); safe only while the phone shows Kick alone (m:followingMerge.ts:L12-L32)
- [ ] The phone polls pinned messages by Twitch channel id while `channel_state` keys the same data by login (m:screens/WatchScreen.tsx:L561-L589)
- [ ] Emote usage tallies conflict on `twitch_user_id,emote_id` with no provider, and the favourite channel read drops the platform (s:services/supabaseService.ts:L1079-L1134, L1180-L1211)
- [ ] Rust side: `ChatHistory::recent_by_user` matches a bare user id across every ring, Kick rings included (r:services/chat_history.rs)

## Part 2: by area

Logic in each area's own `src/mobile` files and the shared or desktop files it follows that are not in Part 1. The area names match the parity contract files in `parity/`.

### Shell, navigation and design system

#### m:boot/useMobileBoot.ts

- [ ] Boot gate state machine: settings read, then auth check, then `isBooting` false in a `finally`, then the root decides booting / needs setup / signed out / main (m:boot/useMobileBoot.ts:L73-L82) (m:MobileApp.tsx:L57-L141) -> new `r:commands/app.rs` command `boot_state` (settings snapshot + auth result + `needs_setup` + sign-in reason) plus a `boot-state-changed` event | callers: desktop d:App.tsx:L897-L910 (parallel allSettled) / Android-mobile m:boot/useMobileBoot.ts, m:MobileApp.tsx | risk: med, the two shells order the steps differently and Android skips every later listener if `load_settings` throws [P1]
- [ ] Drops token validation once per launch after auth (`validate_drops_token`, clears only on a real 401) (m:boot/useMobileBoot.ts:L84-L106) -> run it inside Rust's own post-auth startup task next to `r:commands/drops.rs:L781` | callers: desktop d:App.tsx:L318-L323 (duplicate) / Android-mobile m:boot/useMobileBoot.ts | risk: low, already a single Rust command; delete both TS call sites [P1]
- [ ] Own-account set for cosmetics and identity prewarm: list stored accounts, fall back to the active id when the registry is not ready, append the active id if missing, register them as own, revalidate cosmetics, fetch the full profile, warm identities (m:boot/useMobileBoot.ts:L116-L165) -> `r:services/account_store.rs` (the id list) + a `prewarm_own_identities` task in `r:services/seventv_cosmetics_resolver.rs` / `r:services/profile_cache_service.rs` started after auth | callers: desktop d:App.tsx:L956-L1000 / Android-mobile m:boot/useMobileBoot.ts | risk: med, identity keys: Twitch numeric `user_id` only, 7TV ids resolved inside Rust, never mixed with logins [P1]
- [ ] 7TV session keep-alive policy: 8000 ms after boot, refresh when the JWT expires within 7 days, at most one attempt per 12 h, attempt stamp kept in localStorage `sn-7tv-refresh-attempt` (m:boot/useMobileBoot.ts:L127-L138) (s:services/sevenTvSession.ts:L1-L89) -> `r:services/seventv_auth_service.rs` owns the timer, window and stamp; it asks the host for the hidden sign-in reload through a `host_request` | callers: desktop d:App.tsx:L950-L958 / Android-mobile m:boot/useMobileBoot.ts | risk: med, the re-mint itself needs a host web view; keyed by Twitch user id [P1]
- [ ] Watched-stream-offline matching: compare the event's provider and lower-cased channel login with the stream being watched before ending playback (m:boot/useMobileBoot.ts:L229-L239) -> `r:services/watch_session.rs` emits a targeted `watched-stream-offline` only for the active session | callers: desktop d:App.tsx:L262-L276 / Android-mobile m:boot/useMobileBoot.ts | risk: low, key space is provider + login (Kick slug), never a Twitch numeric id [P1]

### Following, Browse and search

Key spaces used in this area:

- Twitch user id (numeric string): hype trains, watch streaks, collaborations, shared chats, set_home_extra_channels. Twitch only; a Kick numeric id here names an unrelated Twitch channel.
- Stream key (`streamKey`): bare lowercase login for Twitch, `kick:<slug>` for Kick. Used for list dedupe and row identity.
- Rust `card_key` / `favorite_id` (`r:services/providers/key.rs`): what `unified_following` dedupes on. Matches the stream key for Twitch and Kick; differs for YouTube (video id vs UC id).
- Twitch category id, Kick category id: separate spaces, never cross-fed (KickBrowseBody exists because of this).

### Watch screen and player

Key spaces: every item below keys channels by the lowercase Twitch LOGIN (or `provider:channel` for other platforms) unless it says otherwise; the earning gate, drops and channel_state also carry the numeric Twitch broadcaster id. Nothing here touches 7TV ids or chatter user ids.

WebView-only items (they disappear on a native AVPlayer / sample-buffer engine and need no Rust home) are marked `[~] WebView-only`.

#### m:player/useMobileHlsEngine.ts

- [ ] Earning-gate state machine: `playing` reports true at once and cancels a pending pause; `pause`/`ended` report false only after a 6000 ms grace; a pause while hidden (or a pending one at hide) reports at once; teardown cancels and reports nothing; each report chained and abandoned after 2000 ms (m:player/useMobileHlsEngine.ts:L39-L122, m:player/useMobileHlsEngine.ts:L355-L427, m:player/useMobileHlsEngine.ts:L471-L485) -> r:services/watch_heartbeat_service.rs: a `report_player_event {event: playing|pause|ended|hidden|teardown}` command that applies the grace and ordering in Rust (keep `report_player_playing` for MultiNook slots) | callers: desktop d:components/VideoPlayer.tsx (L640-L660, L3173-L3185) / Android-mobile m:player/useMobileHlsEngine.ts | risk: med (a false "paused" costs a whole earning minute; desktop and phone must switch in one change) [P1]
- [ ] Low-latency path decision: asks `get_stream_low_latency` once at build and treats everything else as normal latency (no prefetch probe, so the phone misses desktop's "promotion" path) (m:player/useMobileHlsEngine.ts:L190-L214) -> r:services/ll_origin.rs (or a new new `services/live_latency.rs`): return a `LivePlan {path: ll|promotion|plain, gap_secs, edge_target_secs, gate_threshold_secs, governor_profile}` in `StreamStartResult` so no client probes | callers: desktop d:components/VideoPlayer.tsx (L1094-L1150, which also calls `get_stream_prefetch_present`) / Android-mobile m:player/useMobileHlsEngine.ts | risk: low (pure decision over two relay facts and one setting) [P1]
- [ ] Cold-start buffer gate numbers: start after 3 s buffered (1 s on the parts path), snap to buffered end minus the gap only if more than 0.5 s ahead, 5000 ms give-up timer, muted-autoplay fallback (m:player/useMobileHlsEngine.ts:L265-L307) -> the thresholds into the r: `LivePlan` above; the seek itself stays in the player (AVPlayer in-buffer seek, or the sample-buffer engine's start in Rust) | callers: desktop d:components/VideoPlayer.tsx (L76-L79 snap reserve/window, L1733-L1760) / Android-mobile m:player/useMobileHlsEngine.ts | risk: low; desktop's snap differs (reserve 1.0 s, 8 s window), pick one rule while moving [P1]
- [~] WebView-only: hls.js construction and loader policy (back buffer 30 s, max buffer from `max_buffer_length`, 60 MB cap, hole 0.5 s, nudges, manifest 10 s x 3, level 10 s x 4, fragment 20 s x 6, ABR knobs) (m:player/useMobileHlsEngine.ts:L216-L251) -> none; AVPlayer owns its loader, Rust owns the relay | callers: desktop d:components/VideoPlayer.tsx / Android-mobile m:player/useMobileHlsEngine.ts | risk: low

### Chat view

Key spaces used below: **TW-id** = numeric Twitch user id (bare); **TW-login** = lowercased Twitch login; **room-key** = chat slice key (bare TW-login for Twitch, `provider:slug` lowercased otherwise); **chatter-key** = cosmetics/history key (bare TW-id for Twitch, `provider:id` otherwise); **Kick-id** = numeric Kick user id (same numeric range as TW-id, must never be bare outside Twitch); **7TV-id** = 7TV user or emote id.

### Chat composer and emotes

Key spaces used below: "Twitch user id" = numeric Helix id; "login" = lowercase Twitch login; "room key" = the chat slice key (bare login for Twitch, `kick:slug` / `youtube:id` for others); "emote id" = the provider's emote id. Never mix them.

### Rewards, badges, drops and channel points

Key spaces used below: Twitch user id (numeric string, `channel_id`), Twitch login (lowercased, `channel_login`), badge key `set_id/version` (what Rust's standing uses) versus the gallery tile key `set_id-version`, campaign id, drop id, claim id. None of this area touches Kick or 7TV ids except to exclude Kick streams.

- [ ] Whether a campaign can be earned on a channel: unrestricted earns anywhere in its game; restricted (ACL flag or a non-empty allow-list) needs the lowercased login on the list; restricted with zero parsed channels or an unknown login is never earnable (m:dropsEligibility.ts:L17-L33) -> `impl DropCampaign { fn earnable_on(&self, login: &str) -> bool }` in r:models/drops.rs, used by drops_service, drops_overview and home_snapshot | callers: desktop none (desktop GameDetailPanel and BadgeDetailOverlay read `allowed_channels` with their own rules) / Android-mobile dropsCampaigns.ts, watch/DropProgressBar.tsx, ui/MobileStreamCard.tsx | risk: low, pure predicate; key space Twitch login compared case-insensitively against `allowed_channels[].name` (a display name, so a localized display name that differs from the login would miss: prefer the channel id `allowed_channels[].id` in Rust) [P1]

### Profiles, user cards and cosmetics

Identity key spaces used in this area, named on every line:

- **TW-ID**: bare numeric Twitch user id (chat key for Twitch, cosmetics id, history key, StreamNook member key in Supabase)
- **CHAT-KEY**: chat-space user key (TW-ID bare, `kick:<id>`, `youtube:UC...`)
- **MEMBER-ID**: the TW-ID a StreamNook membership is filed under (CHAT-KEY resolved through member aliases)
- **LOGIN**: lowercase Twitch login; **CHANNEL-LOGIN**: the room's login
- **7TV-ID**: 7TV user ULID (token `sub`, or inventory `seventvUserId`)
- **SLUG**: StreamNook cosmetic slug; **THEME-ID**: atmosphere id, optionally with Cologne `+coin` / `+border` suffixes
- **BADGE-SET**: Twitch badge `set_id` + `version`

### Settings (every panel except Chat)

#### Cross-cutting: settings writes and reset arrows

- [ ] Per-field settings writes: every panel builds a whole group (`video_player`, `streamlink`, `auto_switch`, `cache`, `live_notifications`) from the page's copy and saves it through `patch_settings`, so a stale copy overwrites keys saved elsewhere (d:components/settings/PlayerSettings.tsx:L69-L72, d:components/settings/PlayerSettings.tsx:L107-L119, m:screens/AdFreeSettings.tsx:L84-L91, m:screens/MobileNotificationsSettings.tsx:L140-L151) -> a typed `set_setting(path, value)` read-modify-write command in r:commands/settings.rs beside `set_chat_user_hidden`, emitting the settings-updated event | callers: desktop PlayerSettings.tsx, CacheSettings.tsx, ThemeSettings.tsx, SupportSettings.tsx / Android-mobile AdFreeSettings.tsx, MobileNotificationsSettings.tsx | risk: med (touches every settings write; store echo timing changes) [P1]

### You tab, accounts and sign-in

Proposed new module used below: new `services/sign_in_sheet.rs`, a host-agnostic coordinator for the one in-app sign-in browser (open/close/cancel state, which flow owns it, navigation policy, capture acceptance, deadlines). The Android Kotlin overlay and the iOS WKWebView sheet both become thin hosts that ask it what to do and report what happened. `r:twitch_login_plugin.rs` becomes the Android host arm.

#### Identity key spaces touched by this area

- Twitch user id (bare numeric string): `currentUser.user_id`, the Twitch avatar's cosmetics key, `store_seventv_token.twitchId`, the linked-account claim key, the 7TV re-mint stamp owner.
- Twitch login (lowercased): Subscribe auto-close matching, `/gift` broadcaster check.
- 7TV user id (ULID from the JWT `sub`): `store_seventv_token.userId`, cosmetic writes. Never mixed with a Twitch id.
- Kick account id (numeric, collides with Twitch ids): roster `account_id`, the Kick avatar's cosmetics key (must be `kick:`-namespaced), the linked-account claim value.
- Kick channel slug (lowercased): imported Kick follows in `provider_follows` (r:commands/provider_browse.rs:L361-L397).
